+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: status=resp.status; result=json.load(resp)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err: status=err.code; result=json.load(err)
+    rows.append({'route':route,'text':t,'request':body,'status':status,'seconds':round(time.time()-started,5),'body':result})
+    if result.get('choose'):
+        for choice in result['choose']:
+            req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+'/api/explore',data=json.dumps({'text':choice['id']}).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: clicked=json.load(resp)
+            assert clicked.get('center',{}).get('id')==choice['id'],(t,choice,clicked)
+            rows.append({'route':'/api/explore','text':choice['id'],'from_choice':t,'status':200,'body':clicked})
+    (OUT/'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')

## New should-fix: question parentheses suppress a valid preferred name

At `src/query.py:896-898`, the new boundary expression forbids either parenthesis or square bracket directly beside a shared-key preferred name. This repairs the stereochemical-prefix case, but also removes an ordinary standalone name enclosed by punctuation.

| Exact input | Parent query | Current query / real HTTP |
|---|---|---|
| `What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?` | CHEMBL3; 2 evidence rows | No entity; 0 evidence rows in `/api/ask`, `/api/check`, `/api/structured` |
| `What does (NICOTINE) target?` | CHEMBL3; 2 evidence rows | No entity; 0 evidence rows in all three APIs |
| `What trials evaluate [NICOTINE]?` | CHEMBL3; 2 evidence rows | No entity; 0 evidence rows in all three APIs |
| `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` | CHEMBL3; 2 evidence rows | CHEMBL3; 2 evidence rows in all three APIs |
| `What trials evaluate (osimertinib)?` | CHEMBL3353410 | Still CHEMBL3353410; 12 evidence rows |

The patch should distinguish a stereochemical prefix attached to a name from punctuation enclosing a name. No repair is performed here. Both return zero evidence in all three question APIs. Bare `(+/-)NICOTINE` still links/maps CHEMBL440464 and its own evidence. This closes the wrong-record error by abstention; it does not restore recall of that racemic molecule inside a sentence.

All round-33 blockers and the original round-32 identity blockers are closed. There is a new question recall regression, an important chemical-normalization limitation of the agreed comparison rule, and a separate source-metadata error; these are described below rather than concealed by the approval.

+## New code/design should-fix findings
+
+### M34-S1 — ordinary parentheses or brackets suppress a valid question name
+
+**New, nonblocking question recall regression.** `src/query.py:896-898` excludes parentheses and brackets on either side of a shared-key preferred name. Parent/current comparison and real HTTP show:
+
+| Exact input | Parent | HEAD |
+|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What does (NICOTINE) target?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What trials evaluate [NICOTINE]?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+
+All three question APIs show the loss. Unwrapped `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` still returns two rows; `What trials evaluate (osimertinib)?` still returns 12. A stereochemical prefix attached to a name and punctuation enclosing a name need different treatment. The map correctly leaves these full questions undrawn, so this does not fail the map approval bar. [Parent/current evidence and 45 HTTP calls](contract/boundary_question_comparison.json), [root HTTP corroboration](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+### M34-S2 — the agreed punctuation equivalence can erase stereochemistry
+
+**Nonblocking limitation of the specified `_sig` design, not a newly misfiled alias.** Exact `(+)-NICOTINE` and `(+)NICOTINE` draw **CHEMBL440464, `(+/-)NICOTINE`**. The stored preferred-name record and node label are `(+/-)NICOTINE`; the input is admitted because `_sig` at `src/app.py:261-272` drops the slash and hyphen while retaining `+`, giving `+nicotine` for all three strings. The linker itself returns no entity for the positive-enantiomer forms. [Source record, signatures and HTTP](attacks/stereochemical_punctuation_http.json), [independent root HTTP](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+This is a real chemical distinction: ChEBI identifies `(+)-nicotine` as the R enantiomer, whereas its racemate record contains equal R and S amounts. [ChEBI R-nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:39162), [ChEBI racemate](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:18723). The review does **not** silently interpret signature equality as chemical equivalence. It is nonblocking here because the user explicitly defines admission under `_sig` and disregards punctuation: these inputs satisfy that stated rule and are not linker-refused names. A stronger medicinal-identity guarantee would require changing that comparison policy, preserving stereochemical notation or refusing such normalized matches.
+
+## Data findings, separate from code
+
+### M34-D1 — THEOPHYLLINE is incorrectly typed as Protein upstream
+
+**Nonblocking source metadata error.** Exact input `THEOPHYLLINE` / `Theophylline` draws CHEMBL1355736 with `props.drug_type: "Protein"`. Its raw source is **`data/raw/drugs.json:1`, `data[191].drug`**, containing `id: "CHEMBL1355736"`, `name: "THEOPHYLLINE"`, `drugType: "Protein"`. `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, `dictionary.drugs["CHEMBL1355736"].drug_type`, and the node properties preserve that value. ChEBI independently identifies theophylline as dimethylxanthine, formula C7H8N4O2. [ChEBI identity](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:28177).
+
+The map reproduces the stored value faithfully, and no contrary molecular identity of the named node was established. Therefore this is a curation issue, not a stored-value transcription failure or a wrong-product identity blocker. [Complete source record and response](attacks/theophylline_metadata.json), [HTTP confirmation](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json).
+

+import hashlib, json, os, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims' / 'm34'
+
+def hashes():
+    paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf-8').split('\0')
+    extra = ['data/processed/oncokg.sqlite','data/processed/resolved.json']
+    return {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths + extra if p and (ROOT/p).is_file()}
+
+if sys.argv[1] == 'before':
+    (OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'hashes':hashes()},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'tests':
+    temp = OUT/'tmp'; temp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    env=os.environ.copy(); env.update(PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',TEMP=str(temp),TMP=str(temp),PYTEST_ADDOPTS='--basetemp="'+str(temp/'pytest')+'"')
+    start=time.time()
+    with (OUT/'pytest.txt').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
+        r=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pytest','tests','-q','-p','no:cacheprovider'],cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+    meta={'command':'python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider','returncode':r.returncode,'elapsed_seconds':round(time.time()-start,3)}
+    (OUT/'pytest_meta.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(meta)); print((OUT/'pytest.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'after':
+    before=json.loads((OUT/'integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8')); after=hashes()
+    result={'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'checked_files':len(before['hashes']),'changed':[p for p,h in before['hashes'].items() if after.get(p)!=h],'added':sorted(set(after)-set(before['hashes'])),'tracked_status':subprocess.check_output(['git','status','--short','--untracked-files=no'],cwd=ROOT).decode()}
+    (OUT/'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result))

+import hashlib, json, pathlib, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
+
+ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT=ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m34'
+BASE='http://127.0.0.1:8934'
+old=json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m33/root_http.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+jobs=[(x['route'],x['text']) for x in old]
+extra=['Myocet™','My/o/cet','My ocet','Inf/lectra','Equi/dacent','Fy/arro','Megace/ES','Oraqix','Actikerall','Betnesol-n','Maxitrol','Cipro HC','Tobradex','PRED-G','NEO-DELTA-CORTEF','LEP-ETU','Darzalex','Tepezza','Tevimbra','Winrevair','Privigen','Lmx 4','Kadcyla','Tagrisso','Keytruda','Remicade','Tarceva©','Tarceva℠','(+/-)NICOTINE','NICOTINE','ABT-510','ABT 510','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18','MAGE-A3','MAGEA3','Roche','MET','chembl3353410','nct00200395','ensg00000146648','ORG:roche','CHEMBL440464','CHEMBL3']
+jobs += [('/api/explore',t) for t in extra]
+for t in ['What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?','What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?','What trials evaluate NICOTINE?','Which trials evaluate Tobradex?','Which trials evaluate PRED-G?']:
+    jobs += [(route,t) for route in ['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check']]
+rows=[]
+for route,t in dict.fromkeys(jobs):
+    body={'text':t}
+    if route=='/api/structured': body['answer']='{"claims":[]}'
+    if route=='/api/check': body['answer']='NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    started=time.time()
+    req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+route,data=json.dumps(body).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: status=resp.status; result=json.load(resp)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err: status=err.code; result=json.load(err)
+    rows.append({'route':route,'text':t,'request':body,'status':status,'seconds':round(time.time()-started,5),'body':result})
+    if result.get('choose'):
+        for choice in result['choose']:
+            req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+'/api/explore',data=json.dumps({'text':choice['id']}).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: clicked=json.load(resp)
+            assert clicked.get('center',{}).get('id')==choice['id'],(t,choice,clicked)
+            rows.append({'route':'/api/explore','text':choice['id'],'from_choice':t,'status':200,'body':clicked})
+    (OUT/'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+page=urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=30).read()
+(OUT/'root_served_try.html').write_bytes(page)
+summary={'requests':len(rows),'non_200':[{'text':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'status':r['status']} for r in rows if r['status']!=200], 'served_page_matches_file':page==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes(),'page_sha256':hashlib.sha256(page).hexdigest(), 'cases':[{'input':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'result': next((k for k in ['center','refused','alias_only','choose','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in r['body']), 'question'),'center':r['body'].get('center',{}).get('id'),'choices':[c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose',[])]} for r in rows]}
+(OUT/'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in summary.items() if k!='cases'}))

# Round 34 identity/spec attack lane

Reviewed HEAD `1f6017e8ae7923d21cfafca64b1285331e0d2c18` against parent `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`. No tracked edits and no privately owned server. Actual HTTP requests used the root-owned CLI on port 8934. The scripts use the current implementation; comparisons below are bounded evidence, not proof over every possible input.

## Result

No new map blocker was established in this lane under the expressly specified whole-name `_sig` contract. No current exact preferred-name/node-label/target-symbol/company-key/disease-key record was independently proven to name a different medicinal product from its drawn node. A chemical punctuation limitation and an inherited metadata error are separately documented below.

## Independent source and mutation sweeps

`source_audit.py` reconstructs the admitted source set independently from `dictionary.index`, SQL node labels, target symbols and company/disease keys, with a separately implemented signature function. It does not use `Engine.names` as its oracle. [All source records](all_source_records.json), [all map results](source_sweep.json), [summary](source_summary.json).

- **14,639 distinct source strings**, **6,469 live nodes**: 8,061 centers, 6,407 `alias_only`, 164 `refused`, four `choose`, three `not_found`; **zero** membership, silent-ambiguity, incomplete-choice or refused-stored-signature failures. Direct ID inputs are expressly exempt from name-refusal checks as the specified ID path.
- The **8,063 read-name strings** separately produce 8,054 centers, four choices, two refused tracer names and three retired-company-label `not_found` replies. The refused tracer names are the recorded exceptions: `NM-404 I-124`, `[18F]FLUOROMISONIDAZOLE`. All 1,066 live drug labels and all indexed preferred-name records match the drug dictionary preferred name.
- Only two drug-preferred-name signatures are shared: `ABT 510`/`ABT-510` -> CHEMBL262489/CHEMBL386115, and `FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18`/`FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18` -> CHEMBL2030434/CHEMBL497613. Each spelling returns the two live choices; neither silently selects.
- **164 refused stored spellings × 13 forms = 2,132 inputs**, all `refused`, zero centers. Forms cover plain, trademark/service/registered/copyright signs, internal slash/comma/semicolon/space, interletter spaces, zero-width space, fullwidth letters/digits, combining accent. The same-signature assertion holds for this cohort. [Results](refusal_variants.json), [summary](refusal_variants_summary.json).
- **1,066 drug preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396 inputs**, zero centers: 6,335 `not_one_name`, 49 `not_found`, 12 `refused`. Suffixes are `+`, `$`, `=`, U+2622, Greek gamma U+03B3 and Chinese U+4E2D. [Results](significant_suffixes.json), [summary](significant_suffixes_summary.json).
- **44 HTTP controls** corroborate current refused-product fixes, former brands now `alias_only`, choices, IDs, exact company keys and Unicode behavior. U+200B/U+FEFF and fullwidth letters normalize as intended; U+034F/U+FE0F stay significant and fail closed. [Complete HTTP responses and code points](http_attack_reproductions.json).
- **54 private retirement cases / 10 assertion groups** pass. ABT/FDG both-live names choose; one-retired names draw only the live candidate; all-retired names and upper/lower retired IDs return `not_found`. Real `Engine.__init__` executes both before and after retirement using SQLite memory-copy connection factories. Retired Roche cannot fall back to its folic-acid alias. The production database SHA-256 is unchanged before/after. [Fixture script](retired_choices.py), [results](retired_private_fixture.json).

The map scan completed in 488.67 seconds while other audit work ran. This is not an isolated performance measurement.

## Stored-alias scope qualification (nonblocking)

The new comment says every other stored drug name goes to `alias_only`, but `src/app.py:61-65` actually traverses the curated/deduplicated `dictionary.index`, not all `dictionary.drugs[...].aliases` records. There are **1,652 alias records whose exact spelling is absent** from that source universe. Of these, **427 records / 247 signatures have no equivalent indexed/read spelling at all**; their direct replies are 356 `not_found`, 59 `not_one_name`, 12 `refused`, with zero centers. [All discrepancies](source_discrepancies.json), [absent signatures and actual replies](absent_alias_signatures.json).

These counts include deliberately curated class names and short forms, so they are **not 247 proven defects** and must not be presented as missing safe drug identities. Concrete non-center examples include `(+)-artemisinin` -> `not_one_name`, `tα1` -> `not_found`, `epoetin β` -> `not_found`. This qualifies the blanket reply description; it does not invalidate preferred-name-only admission. The 2,132-case refusal sweep is explicitly bounded to indexed names, node labels, target symbols and company/disease keys. The root lane independently assesses any practical missing-refusal consequences from omitted alias records.

## Chemical punctuation design limitation (nonblocking under this brief)

Exact HTTP inputs **`(+)-NICOTINE`** and **`(+)NICOTINE`** draw **CHEMBL440464, `(+/-)NICOTINE`**. The ordinary input linker returns an empty entity list. `_sig` at `src/app.py:269-272` drops ASCII minus and slash as punctuation while retaining plus, so all three become `+nicotine`. [HTTP responses, signatures, code points, raw source records](stereochemical_punctuation_http.json).

The source preferred record is `data/raw/drugs.json` and normalized `dictionary.drugs.CHEMBL440464.name = "(+/-)NICOTINE"`; its preferred alias has type `preferred_name`, source `null`. ChEBI explicitly identifies **`(+)-nicotine` as the R enantiomer**, whereas its separate racemate record is an equal mixture of R and S. These are chemically different identities: [ChEBI R-nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:39162), [ChEBI racemic nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:18723). PubChem connects CHEMBL440464 with the racemic-name record: [PubChem CID 942](https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/compound/22083-74-5).

This is **not a misfiled preferred-name record** and **not a `_sig` membership violation**: the requested comparison expressly discards punctuation, and the map follows it. It is an inherited semantic limitation of that comparison and a should-fix design consideration if chemical identity is to be stronger than the present contract. The corresponding control `(+/−)NICOTINE` using Unicode minus U+2212 returns `not_one_name`; `(±)NICOTINE` returns `not_found`. Do not count it as the repaired M33-S1 question-linker regression.

## Separate data metadata problem (nonblocking)

Exact HTTP input **`THEOPHYLLINE`** correctly draws **CHEMBL1355736**, but its displayed `props.drug_type` is **`Protein`**. This is already in `data/raw/drugs.json:1`, pointer **`data[191].drug`**, as `id: CHEMBL1355736`, `name: THEOPHYLLINE`, `drugType: Protein`; it persists through `dictionary.drugs.CHEMBL1355736.drug_type` and the SQL node props. [Exact raw/dictionary/node records and actual HTTP](theophylline_metadata.json).

[ChEBI CHEBI:28177](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:28177) identifies theophylline as a dimethylxanthine, formula C7H8N4O2. That supports a source **classification error**, not an inference that the wrong substance was drawn. The page transcribes the stored value accurately; it does not fail the stored-value guarantee. The reviewed commit changes neither this source record nor its type. [Independent reference notes and screened counterevidence](independent_identity_references.json).

## Spec axis (under 400 words)

The primary contract in `m34/brief.md:29-31` asks for a center only when the whole input is one read name under `_sig`, no wrong-product read record, and no spelling bypass of a stored refusal. The independent 14,639-source-string scan found zero structural violations, while 2,132 refusal mutations all refused and 6,396 significant-suffix attacks all remained undrawn. The source change at `src/app.py:61-76` implements the requested preferred-name/node-label/target-symbol/company-key/disease-key admission. The refusal loop at `src/app.py:149-154` closes the exercised M33-B1 variants. Four ambiguity responses preserve both live drug IDs.

Two limitations do not overturn this result. The wording “Every other stored name … goes into alias_only” (`m34/brief.md:16`) is broader than the indexed source actually used; deliberately curated and deduplicated aliases can instead be `not_found` or `not_one_name`. The chemical `(+)-NICOTINE`/`(+/-)NICOTINE` collision follows the expressly allowed punctuation equivalence, so it is a design limitation rather than an implementation divergence. The inherited THEOPHYLLINE type anomaly is a separately sourced metadata problem; no different product or differing stored value was established.

Historical exact dispositions, question-box/demo regressions, UI markup, private retirement fixtures and A–G are consolidated by the root and the other independent lanes. No unrelated scope expansion or production repair was made.

# Historical cost reproductions — M34

Fifteen copied suite executions, including the isotope-instrumentation supplement, exited 0. The original2,000-piece answer was additionally exercised against the root CLI. Each reproduction and its disposition are below. Timings overlapped other audit work, so these are descriptive latencies, not an isolated benchmark or evidence of a new regression. Historical `isolated_` filenames do not imply process/host isolation. No timeout or wrong map is established by these performance checks; residual costs are should-fix items.

The implementation still runs the linker before the one-name test. Inputs over500 characters are tested as the question answer field or directly, not as successful map inputs. Shared API locking means slow question checks can delay valid maps.

All round-33 blockers and the original round-32 identity blockers are closed. +## New code/design should-fix findings
+
+### M34-S1 — ordinary parentheses or brackets suppress a valid question name
+
+**New, nonblocking question recall regression.** `src/query.py:896-898` excludes parentheses and brackets on either side of a shared-key preferred name. Parent/current comparison and real HTTP show:
+
+| Exact input | Parent | HEAD |
+|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What does (NICOTINE) target?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What trials evaluate [NICOTINE]?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+
+All three question APIs show the loss. Unwrapped `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` still returns two rows; `What trials evaluate (osimertinib)?` still returns 12. A stereochemical prefix attached to a name and punctuation enclosing a name need different treatment. The map correctly leaves these full questions undrawn, so this does not fail the map approval bar. [Parent/current evidence and 45 HTTP calls](contract/boundary_question_comparison.json), [root HTTP corroboration](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+### M34-S2 — the agreed punctuation equivalence can erase stereochemistry
+
+**Nonblocking limitation of the specified `_sig` design, not a newly misfiled alias.** Exact `(+)-NICOTINE` and `(+)NICOTINE` draw **CHEMBL440464, `(+/-)NICOTINE`**. The stored preferred-name record and node label are `(+/-)NICOTINE`; the input is admitted because `_sig` at `src/app.py:261-272` drops the slash and hyphen while retaining `+`, giving `+nicotine` for all three strings. The linker itself returns no entity for the positive-enantiomer forms. [Source record, signatures and HTTP](attacks/stereochemical_punctuation_http.json), [independent root HTTP](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+This is a real chemical distinction: ChEBI identifies `(+)-nicotine` as the R enantiomer, whereas its racemate record contains equal R and S amounts. [ChEBI R-nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:39162), [ChEBI racemate](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:18723). The review does **not** silently interpret signature equality as chemical equivalence. It is nonblocking here because the user explicitly defines admission under `_sig` and disregards punctuation: these inputs satisfy that stated rule and are not linker-refused names. A stronger medicinal-identity guarantee would require changing that comparison policy, preserving stereochemical notation or refusing such normalized matches.
+
+## Data findings, separate from code
+
+### M34-D1 — THEOPHYLLINE is incorrectly typed as Protein upstream
+
+**Nonblocking source metadata error.** Exact input `THEOPHYLLINE` / `Theophylline` draws CHEMBL1355736 with `props.drug_type: "Protein"`. Its raw source is **`data/raw/drugs.json:1`, `data[191].drug`**, containing `id: "CHEMBL1355736"`, `name: "THEOPHYLLINE"`, `drugType: "Protein"`. `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, `dictionary.drugs["CHEMBL1355736"].drug_type`, and the node properties preserve that value. ChEBI independently identifies theophylline as dimethylxanthine, formula C7H8N4O2. [ChEBI identity](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:28177).
+
+The map reproduces the stored value faithfully, and no contrary molecular identity of the named node was established. Therefore this is a curation issue, not a stored-value transcription failure or a wrong-product identity blocker. [Complete source record and response](attacks/theophylline_metadata.json), [HTTP confirmation](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json).
+

+import hashlib, json, os, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims' / 'm34'
+
+def hashes():
+    paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf-8').split('\0')
+    extra = ['data/processed/oncokg.sqlite','data/processed/resolved.json']
+    return {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths + extra if p and (ROOT/p).is_file()}
+
+if sys.argv[1] == 'before':
+    (OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'hashes':hashes()},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'tests':
+    temp = OUT/'tmp'; temp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    env=os.environ.copy(); env.update(PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',TEMP=str(temp),TMP=str(temp),PYTEST_ADDOPTS='--basetemp="'+str(temp/'pytest')+'"')
+    start=time.time()
+    with (OUT/'pytest.txt').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
+        r=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pytest','tests','-q','-p','no:cacheprovider'],cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+    meta={'command':'python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider','returncode':r.returncode,'elapsed_seconds':round(time.time()-start,3)}
+    (OUT/'pytest_meta.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(meta)); print((OUT/'pytest.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'after':
+    before=json.loads((OUT/'integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8')); after=hashes()
+    result={'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'checked_files':len(before['hashes']),'changed':[p for p,h in before['hashes'].items() if after.get(p)!=h],'added':sorted(set(after)-set(before['hashes'])),'tracked_status':subprocess.check_output(['git','status','--short','--untracked-files=no'],cwd=ROOT).decode()}
+    (OUT/'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result))

+import hashlib, json, pathlib, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
+
+ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT=ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m34'
+BASE='http://127.0.0.1:8934'
+old=json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m33/root_http.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+jobs=[(x['route'],x['text']) for x in old]
+extra=['Myocet™','My/o/cet','My ocet','Inf/lectra','Equi/dacent','Fy/arro','Megace/ES','Oraqix','Actikerall','Betnesol-n','Maxitrol','Cipro HC','Tobradex','PRED-G','NEO-DELTA-CORTEF','LEP-ETU','Darzalex','Tepezza','Tevimbra','Winrevair','Privigen','Lmx 4','Kadcyla','Tagrisso','Keytruda','Remicade','Tarceva©','Tarceva℠','(+/-)NICOTINE','NICOTINE','ABT-510','ABT 510','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18','MAGE-A3','MAGEA3','Roche','MET','chembl3353410','nct00200395','ensg00000146648','ORG:roche','CHEMBL440464','CHEMBL3']
+jobs += [('/api/explore',t) for t in extra]
+for t in ['What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?','What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?','What trials evaluate NICOTINE?','Which trials evaluate Tobradex?','Which trials evaluate PRED-G?']:
+    jobs += [(route,t) for route in ['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check']]
+rows=[]
+for route,t in dict.fromkeys(jobs):
+    body={'text':t}
+    if route=='/api/structured': body['answer']='{"claims":[]}'
+    if route=='/api/check': body['answer']='NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    started=time.time()
+    req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+route,data=json.dumps(body).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: status=resp.status; result=json.load(resp)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err: status=err.code; result=json.load(err)
+    rows.append({'route':route,'text':t,'request':body,'status':status,'seconds':round(time.time()-started,5),'body':result})
+    if result.get('choose'):
+        for choice in result['choose']:
+            req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+'/api/explore',data=json.dumps({'text':choice['id']}).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: clicked=json.load(resp)
+            assert clicked.get('center',{}).get('id')==choice['id'],(t,choice,clicked)
+            rows.append({'route':'/api/explore','text':choice['id'],'from_choice':t,'status':200,'body':clicked})
+    (OUT/'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+page=urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=30).read()
+(OUT/'root_served_try.html').write_bytes(page)
+summary={'requests':len(rows),'non_200':[{'text':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'status':r['status']} for r in rows if r['status']!=200], 'served_page_matches_file':page==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes(),'page_sha256':hashlib.sha256(page).hexdigest(), 'cases':[{'input':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'result': next((k for k in ['center','refused','alias_only','choose','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in r['body']), 'question'),'center':r['body'].get('center',{}).get('id'),'choices':[c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose',[])]} for r in rows]}
+(OUT/'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in summary.items() if k!='cases'}))

Compared with M33's ledger, three more historical recall rows are marked NOT FIXED because their formerly admitted synonyms are now excluded. This is not a new wrong-node blocker. Cohorts overlap and must not be added into unique-defect totals.

Three inherited question-only findings remain NOT FIXED in fresh controls: prefixed `ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH` questions read CHEMBL4297747 ENDOSTATIN, prefixed `Rapamycin, 42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-` questions read CHEMBL413 SIROLIMUS, and the recorded expanded-negation/labelled-control strings lose evidence. The first two belong to one historical row. The map keeps these longer inputs undrawn. All 13 M31-B1 exact inputs remain undrawn with zero evidence in both original wrappers. All six M31 clinical/prefix-ADC recall controls recover evidence arrays identical to their bare-drug controls. [Fresh 116-question controls](qa_controls34.json).

The independent current-source corpus includes **4,660 unique inputs**: every one of the previous **4,631** inputs plus 29 M33/additional inputs. Current results: **295 center, 1,425 refused, 2,556 alias_only, 321 not_one_name, 59 not_found, four choose**. An independently reconstructed index (preferred-name records, live node labels, target symbols, and company/disease keys gated by exact signature/key equality) found **zero whole-read-name/id membership failures, zero silent ambiguous draws, zero choice-set mismatches, zero exceptions**. [Summary](corpus_summary.json), [all responses](corpus_results.json), [implementation of the audit oracle](corpus34.py). The 1,337 previously drawn strings newly excluded across historical finding cohorts are explicitly listed per row; no safety FIXED claim is used to imply restored alias recall.

The four choices are the two ABT-510 spellings and two FDG spellings. Former `Roche`/`MET` drug aliases are excluded, leaving the company/target respectively. The source-index check proves admitted membership and candidate selection, not medicinal-product identity.

## Original-script reuse and additional HTTP coverage

The historical replay copies preserve source SHA-256 hashes and destination hashes in [reuse_manifest.json](reuse_manifest.json). Adaptations are limited to m34 scratch locations, owned ports **18000–18099**, and the root CLI port **8934**. Scripts read current production modules/data; some intentionally load an older query module with current surrounding dependencies. Those comparisons are not a fully restored historical environment.

The exact saved M22 scan adapter is included from the start and replays all 58 saved strings. The original standalone generator is still unavailable and is not claimed to have run. M27/M29 use their original attack artifacts because the rounds had no completed reports. Old positive-center assertions are not the current approval oracle: the independent preferred-name corpus determines current safety dispositions.

An additional **129** report-derived HTTP requests include all 26 Unicode Pd spellings and both original oversized requests. Every dash-label variant is undrawn, and both oversized requests return 400. Twenty-three code/annotation spans are explicitly excluded rather than misreported as exact product inputs. [Supplement](report_input_supplement.json). The M32, M33, 456-brand and supplement HTTP cohorts overlap and their counts must not be added as unique-input coverage.

All **123 historical leaf jobs exited 0**, with no unresolved execution failure. The **151** prepared-source entries are not an execution count. **45** lifecycle records retain original shutdown/integrity fields; none of their recorded port/database/page equality flags is false, and all **27** distinct owned server ports were independently confirmed closed. Not every old lifecycle schema records every field, so this is not 45 complete integrity proofs. [Execution summary](execution_summary.json), [per-job execution](execution34.json), [lifecycle records](lifecycle_records.json).

The original **7,511-key** alias sweep completed. The **1,648-question** frozen and historical replays each report 1,648 identical / zero semantic changes / zero errors. The separate historical no-relink adapter records 72 identical / 1,552 entity-metadata-only / 24 semantic differences / zero errors against its constructed old baseline; those are not HEAD-versus-parent regression counts. [Historical QA summaries](historical_qa_summary.json). Root owns final repository/database integrity verification and final verdict.

## Limits

No new blocker is established by this lane. The map safety oracle is source-membership based and cannot prove that an admitted preferred name denotes the correct real product. Fresh data-identity attacks, current-parent question/demo comparisons, and full DB/UI/server/cost checks belong to other lanes. No visual-browser claim is made here.

All new files and subprocess temporary paths from this lane are under `audit_evidence_claims/m34/replay/`; production files were not edited. Python bytecode was disabled for executed scripts. No source-data repair is claimed.
"""Isolate no-relink on all aliases whose link result differs plus company/mixed questions."""
import ast,json,sqlite3,sys,textwrap,types
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path('src').resolve()))
import query
from app import Engine
OUT=Path('audit_evidence_claims/m34/replay/replay')
cur=Engine(); old=Engine()
src=Path('src/query.py').read_text(encoding='utf8')
method=next(n for n in ast.walk(ast.parse(src)) if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='_link')
assert '(refused | matched)' in ast.get_source_segment(src,method)
ns=dict(query.__dict__)
exec(compile(textwrap.dedent(ast.get_source_segment(src,method)).replace('(refused | matched)','refused'),'HEAD-with-relink','exec'),ns)
old.kg._link=types.MethodType(ns['_link'],old.kg)
for e in (cur,old):
    e.kg.conn.close();e.kg.conn=sqlite3.connect('file:data/processed/oncokg.sqlite?mode=ro',uri=True);e.kg.conn.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
aliases=json.loads(OUT.joinpath('names_attack.json').read_text(encoding='utf8'))['multi_token_aliases']
changed=[r['input'] for r in aliases if r['current']!=r['relink']]
inputs=[template.format(name) for name in changed for template in ['Tell me about {}','Which trials evaluate {}?','Who sponsors {}?','What targets does {} act on?']]
inputs += [template.format(name) for name in ['AstraZeneca','National Cancer Institute','Bristol Myers Squibb','Johnson & Johnson','Pfizer','GlaxoSmithKline','MD Anderson Cancer Center','GSK 1120212 and EGFR','osimertinib and erlotinib hydrochloride','erlotinib hydrochloride and osimertinib','AstraZeneca and erlotinib hydrochloride','erlotinib hydrochloride and AstraZeneca'] for template in ['Tell me about {}','Which trials evaluate {}?','Who sponsors {}?','What targets does {} act on?']]
inputs=json.loads(Path('audit_evidence_claims/m5/names_qa_relink_sweep.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['inputs']
rows=[]
counts={'questions':len(inputs),'identical':0,'entities_only':0,'semantic_changes':0,'errors':0}
for s in inputs:
    try:
        a,b=cur.ask(s),old.ask(s)
        # Effects on answer/evidence/intent matter separately from pruned entity metadata.
        sema={k:v for k,v in a.items() if k!='entities'}
        semb={k:v for k,v in b.items() if k!='entities'}
        kind='identical' if a==b else 'entities_only' if sema==semb else 'semantic_changes'
        counts[kind]+=1
        if kind=='semantic_changes':rows.append({'input':s,'current':a,'relink':b})
    except Exception as ex:
        counts['errors']+=1;rows.append({'input':s,'error':repr(ex)})
out={'baseline':'HEAD identical except _link matched-token exclusion disabled; outer resolver and whole-name guards intact','summary':counts,'inputs':inputs,'changed_answers':rows,'input_source':'audit_evidence_claims/m5/names_qa_relink_sweep.json inputs (frozen exact prior cases)'}
OUT.joinpath('adapted_frozen_qa_relink_sweep.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
print(json.dumps(counts))
for r in rows:
    if 'error' in r:print(r);continue
    print(r['input'],r['current']['intent'],'<-',r['relink']['intent'],len(r['current']['evidence']),len(r['relink']['evidence']))
for e in (cur,old):e.kg.conn.close()

+## New code/design should-fix findings
+
+### M34-S1 — ordinary parentheses or brackets suppress a valid question name
+
+**New, nonblocking question recall regression.** `src/query.py:896-898` excludes parentheses and brackets on either side of a shared-key preferred name. Parent/current comparison and real HTTP show:
+
+| Exact input | Parent | HEAD |
+|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What does (NICOTINE) target?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What trials evaluate [NICOTINE]?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+
+All three question APIs show the loss. Unwrapped `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` still returns two rows; `What trials evaluate (osimertinib)?` still returns 12. A stereochemical prefix attached to a name and punctuation enclosing a name need different treatment. The map correctly leaves these full questions undrawn, so this does not fail the map approval bar. [Parent/current evidence and 45 HTTP calls](contract/boundary_question_comparison.json), [root HTTP corroboration](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+### M34-S2 — the agreed punctuation equivalence can erase stereochemistry
+
+**Nonblocking limitation of the specified `_sig` design, not a newly misfiled alias.** Exact `(+)-NICOTINE` and `(+)NICOTINE` draw **CHEMBL440464, `(+/-)NICOTINE`**. The stored preferred-name record and node label are `(+/-)NICOTINE`; the input is admitted because `_sig` at `src/app.py:261-272` drops the slash and hyphen while retaining `+`, giving `+nicotine` for all three strings. The linker itself returns no entity for the positive-enantiomer forms. [Source record, signatures and HTTP](attacks/stereochemical_punctuation_http.json), [independent root HTTP](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+This is a real chemical distinction: ChEBI identifies `(+)-nicotine` as the R enantiomer, whereas its racemate record contains equal R and S amounts. [ChEBI R-nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:39162), [ChEBI racemate](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:18723). The review does **not** silently interpret signature equality as chemical equivalence. It is nonblocking here because the user explicitly defines admission under `_sig` and disregards punctuation: these inputs satisfy that stated rule and are not linker-refused names. A stronger medicinal-identity guarantee would require changing that comparison policy, preserving stereochemical notation or refusing such normalized matches.
+
+## Data findings, separate from code
+
+### M34-D1 — THEOPHYLLINE is incorrectly typed as Protein upstream
+
+**Nonblocking source metadata error.** Exact input `THEOPHYLLINE` / `Theophylline` draws CHEMBL1355736 with `props.drug_type: "Protein"`. Its raw source is **`data/raw/drugs.json:1`, `data[191].drug`**, containing `id: "CHEMBL1355736"`, `name: "THEOPHYLLINE"`, `drugType: "Protein"`. `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, `dictionary.drugs["CHEMBL1355736"].drug_type`, and the node properties preserve that value. ChEBI independently identifies theophylline as dimethylxanthine, formula C7H8N4O2. [ChEBI identity](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:28177).
+
+The map reproduces the stored value faithfully, and no contrary molecular identity of the named node was established. Therefore this is a curation issue, not a stored-value transcription failure or a wrong-product identity blocker. [Complete source record and response](attacks/theophylline_metadata.json), [HTTP confirmation](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json).
+

+import hashlib, json, os, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims' / 'm34'
+
+def hashes():
+    paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf-8').split('\0')
+    extra = ['data/processed/oncokg.sqlite','data/processed/resolved.json']
+    return {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths + extra if p and (ROOT/p).is_file()}
+
+if sys.argv[1] == 'before':
+    (OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'hashes':hashes()},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'tests':
+    temp = OUT/'tmp'; temp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    env=os.environ.copy(); env.update(PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',TEMP=str(temp),TMP=str(temp),PYTEST_ADDOPTS='--basetemp="'+str(temp/'pytest')+'"')
+    start=time.time()
+    with (OUT/'pytest.txt').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
+        r=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pytest','tests','-q','-p','no:cacheprovider'],cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+    meta={'command':'python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider','returncode':r.returncode,'elapsed_seconds':round(time.time()-start,3)}
+    (OUT/'pytest_meta.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(meta)); print((OUT/'pytest.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'after':
+    before=json.loads((OUT/'integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8')); after=hashes()
+    result={'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'checked_files':len(before['hashes']),'changed':[p for p,h in before['hashes'].items() if after.get(p)!=h],'added':sorted(set(after)-set(before['hashes'])),'tracked_status':subprocess.check_output(['git','status','--short','--untracked-files=no'],cwd=ROOT).decode()}
+    (OUT/'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result))

+import hashlib, json, pathlib, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
+
+ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT=ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m34'
+BASE='http://127.0.0.1:8934'
+old=json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m33/root_http.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+jobs=[(x['route'],x['text']) for x in old]
+extra=['Myocet™','My/o/cet','My ocet','Inf/lectra','Equi/dacent','Fy/arro','Megace/ES','Oraqix','Actikerall','Betnesol-n','Maxitrol','Cipro HC','Tobradex','PRED-G','NEO-DELTA-CORTEF','LEP-ETU','Darzalex','Tepezza','Tevimbra','Winrevair','Privigen','Lmx 4','Kadcyla','Tagrisso','Keytruda','Remicade','Tarceva©','Tarceva℠','(+/-)NICOTINE','NICOTINE','ABT-510','ABT 510','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18','MAGE-A3','MAGEA3','Roche','MET','chembl3353410','nct00200395','ensg00000146648','ORG:roche','CHEMBL440464','CHEMBL3']
+jobs += [('/api/explore',t) for t in extra]
+for t in ['What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?','What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?','What trials evaluate NICOTINE?','Which trials evaluate Tobradex?','Which trials evaluate PRED-G?']:
+    jobs += [(route,t) for route in ['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check']]
+rows=[]
+for route,t in dict.fromkeys(jobs):
+    body={'text':t}
+    if route=='/api/structured': body['answer']='{"claims":[]}'
+    if route=='/api/check': body['answer']='NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    started=time.time()
+    req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+route,data=json.dumps(body).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: status=resp.status; result=json.load(resp)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err: status=err.code; result=json.load(err)
+    rows.append({'route':route,'text':t,'request':body,'status':status,'seconds':round(time.time()-started,5),'body':result})
+    if result.get('choose'):
+        for choice in result['choose']:
+            req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+'/api/explore',data=json.dumps({'text':choice['id']}).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: clicked=json.load(resp)
+            assert clicked.get('center',{}).get('id')==choice['id'],(t,choice,clicked)
+            rows.append({'route':'/api/explore','text':choice['id'],'from_choice':t,'status':200,'body':clicked})
+    (OUT/'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+page=urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=30).read()
+(OUT/'root_served_try.html').write_bytes(page)
+summary={'requests':len(rows),'non_200':[{'text':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'status':r['status']} for r in rows if r['status']!=200], 'served_page_matches_file':page==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes(),'page_sha256':hashlib.sha256(page).hexdigest(), 'cases':[{'input':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'result': next((k for k in ['center','refused','alias_only','choose','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in r['body']), 'question'),'center':r['body'].get('center',{}).get('id'),'choices':[c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose',[])]} for r in rows]}
+(OUT/'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in summary.items() if k!='cases'}))

+# Independent relation-map review — round 34
+
+Reviewed **HEAD `1f6017e8ae7923d21cfafca64b1285331e0d2c18`**, parent `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`, on 2026-11-04. **CHANGES REQUIRED: one code blocker.** The original round-33 and round-32 blocker reproductions are closed, but the new refusal check omits stored aliases removed from the lookup index. `Cabozantinib (s)-malate` is refused, while `cabozantinibsmalate` and even `Cabozantinib (s)-malate?` draw its node under the same `_sig`. This violates the explicit requirement that every matching refused stored name remains refused. Separate nonblocking findings concern question recall, chemical punctuation equivalence and source metadata.

The prior report and repair notes were reviewed. A FIXED disposition closes the exact reproduction under this policy; it does not mean that upstream records were curated or excluded aliases regained recall. No tracked file was edited.

+## Code blocker
+
+### M34-B1 — a refused stored alias omitted from the index escapes the refusal closure
+
+The exact stored alias **`Cabozantinib (s)-malate`** is present in `data/processed/resolved.json:1` at **`dictionary.drugs.CHEMBL2103868.aliases[2]`**, with `type: "synonym"`, `source: "ChEMBL"`. Its raw provenance is `data/raw/drugs.json:1`, **`data[563].drug.synonyms[1].label`**. The canonical index keeps only the preferred spelling **`CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE`** for this drug/key. `src/normalize.py:165-171` retains the synonym in the drug's alias list but admits only the first same-drug entry into the index.
+
+`Engine.__init__` at **`src/app.py:61-65`** builds both name tables only from that index. Consequently the stored-spelling check at **`src/app.py:149-154`** never asks the linker about the omitted alias. The ordinary input check still refuses its exact spelling, but other spellings reach the preferred-name admission path.
+
+| Exact HTTP input | Current result |
+|---|---|
+| `Cabozantinib (s)-malate` | `refused`, label `Cabozantinib -malate`, reason `names a distinct product or tracer, not the originator molecule` |
+| `cabozantinibsmalate` | **center CHEMBL2103868 — CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE** |
+| `CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE` | **same center** |
+| `Cabozantinib (s)-malate?` | **same center** |
+| `C a b o z a n t i n i b   ( s ) - m a l a t e` | **same center** |
+| `Cabozantinib/(s)-malate` | **same center** |
+| `Cabozantinib\u200b(s)-malate` (actual U+200B, not literal backslash text) | **same center** |
+
+Every name in the table has `_sig == "cabozantinibsmalate"`. The correct literal trademark control `Cabozantinib (s)-malate™` remains refused, and direct ID `CHEMBL2103868` draws the requested ID as allowed. [Independent full HTTP/source confirmation, exact code points](root_blocker_confirmation.json), [initial independent discovery](contract/omitted_raw_refusals.json), [full omitted-alias scan](attacks/omitted_alias_refusal_summary.json), [all follow-up probes](attacks/omitted_alias_refusal_probes.json).
+
+The full supplement examines **1,652 omitted alias occurrences / 1,376 unique spellings**. Eighteen omitted spellings are linker-refused; six have an indexed equivalent. Of 48 targeted variants, five draw, all in this one Cabozantinib failure family; the root's additional question-mark variant also draws. These counts are not separate blockers. The initial discovery's question-mark input is recorded literally; no converted trademark is misreported as a trademark bypass.
+
+This is a **code/spec consistency failure**, not a claim that the salt name is a different chemical product or that this linker refusal is scientifically correct. The brief's rule is unconditional for stored refused names, and no Cabozantinib exception appears in the recorded exclusions. Repair the refusal-equivalence source so it includes all stored alias spellings, including canonical duplicates omitted from the lookup index, while preserving preferred-name-only admission. No production repair was made.
+

+## New code/design should-fix findings
+
+### M34-S1 — ordinary parentheses or brackets suppress a valid question name
+
+**New, nonblocking question recall regression.** `src/query.py:896-898` excludes parentheses and brackets on either side of a shared-key preferred name. Parent/current comparison and real HTTP show:
+
+| Exact input | Parent | HEAD |
+|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What does (NICOTINE) target?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What trials evaluate [NICOTINE]?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+
+All three question APIs show the loss. Unwrapped `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` still returns two rows; `What trials evaluate (osimertinib)?` still returns 12. A stereochemical prefix attached to a name and punctuation enclosing a name need different treatment. The map correctly leaves these full questions undrawn, so this does not fail the map approval bar. [Parent/current evidence and 45 HTTP calls](contract/boundary_question_comparison.json), [root HTTP corroboration](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+### M34-S2 — the agreed punctuation equivalence can erase stereochemistry
+
+**Nonblocking limitation of the specified `_sig` design, not a newly misfiled alias.** Exact `(+)-NICOTINE` and `(+)NICOTINE` draw **CHEMBL440464, `(+/-)NICOTINE`**. The stored preferred-name record and node label are `(+/-)NICOTINE`; the input is admitted because `_sig` at `src/app.py:261-272` drops the slash and hyphen while retaining `+`, giving `+nicotine` for all three strings. The linker itself returns no entity for the positive-enantiomer forms. [Source record, signatures and HTTP](attacks/stereochemical_punctuation_http.json), [independent root HTTP](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+This is a real chemical distinction: ChEBI identifies `(+)-nicotine` as the R enantiomer, whereas its racemate record contains equal R and S amounts. [ChEBI R-nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:39162), [ChEBI racemate](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:18723). The review does **not** interpret signature equality as chemical equivalence. This review classifies it as nonblocking under the literal `_sig` admission criterion: these inputs satisfy the stated punctuation equivalence and are not linker-refused names. That is a scope judgment, **not an explicitly recorded stereochemistry exception**. A stronger medicinal-identity guarantee would require changing that comparison policy, preserving stereochemical notation or refusing such normalized matches.
+
+## Data findings, separate from code

+### M34-D1 — THEOPHYLLINE is incorrectly typed as Protein upstream

+**Nonblocking source metadata error.** Exact input `THEOPHYLLINE` / `Theophylline` draws CHEMBL1355736 with `props.drug_type: "Protein"`. Its raw source is **`data/raw/drugs.json:1`, `data[191].drug`**, containing `id: "CHEMBL1355736"`, `name: "THEOPHYLLINE"`, `drugType: "Protein"`. `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, `dictionary.drugs["CHEMBL1355736"].drug_type`, and the node properties preserve that value. ChEBI independently identifies theophylline as dimethylxanthine, formula C7H8N4O2. [ChEBI identity](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:28177).

+The map reproduces the stored value faithfully, and no contrary molecular identity of the named node was established. Therefore this is a curation issue, not a stored-value transcription failure or a wrong-product identity blocker. [Complete source record and response](attacks/theophylline_metadata.json), [HTTP confirmation](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json).

All 27 owned replay ports are closed. The missing original M22 generator remains covered by its saved exact 58-input adapter; the unavailable generator is not claimed to have run. [Historical contract ledger](contract/HISTORY.md). **15 cost suites exited 0**; their 11 dispositions are six FIXED and five inherited residual costs. The 400-distinct-`isa`, 4,000-U+FDFA, punctuation-token, 1,000-`isa`, and joined-item workloads retain overhead/lock delay. No new timeout or wrong map was established. [Cost ledger](contract/cost/FINDINGS.md). Counts overlap across ledgers and are not added as independent findings. Old positive-map assertions are not substituted for the current admission contract.

+import hashlib, json, os, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims' / 'm34'
+
+def hashes():
+    paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf-8').split('\0')
+    extra = ['data/processed/oncokg.sqlite','data/processed/resolved.json']
+    return {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths + extra if p and (ROOT/p).is_file()}
+
+if sys.argv[1] == 'before':
+    (OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'hashes':hashes()},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'tests':
+    temp = OUT/'tmp'; temp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    env=os.environ.copy(); env.update(PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',TEMP=str(temp),TMP=str(temp),PYTEST_ADDOPTS='--basetemp="'+str(temp/'pytest')+'"')
+    start=time.time()
+    with (OUT/'pytest.txt').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
+        r=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pytest','tests','-q','-p','no:cacheprovider'],cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+    meta={'command':'python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider','returncode':r.returncode,'elapsed_seconds':round(time.time()-start,3)}
+    (OUT/'pytest_meta.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(meta)); print((OUT/'pytest.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'after':
+    before=json.loads((OUT/'integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8')); after=hashes()
+    result={'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'checked_files':len(before['hashes']),'changed':[p for p,h in before['hashes'].items() if after.get(p)!=h],'added':sorted(set(after)-set(before['hashes'])),'tracked_status':subprocess.check_output(['git','status','--short','--untracked-files=no'],cwd=ROOT).decode()}
+    (OUT/'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result))

+result={'head':'1f6017e8ae7923d21cfafca64b1285331e0d2c18','source_file':'data/processed/resolved.json:1','source_records':source,'same_signature_index_records':indexed,'raw_source_file':'data/raw/drugs.json:1','raw_records':raw_records,'read_names':e.names.get(sig),'alias_only_names':e.alias_only.get(sig),'rows':rows,'classification':'Code contract inconsistency: omitted stored spelling is refused, while equivalent names draw; not evidence of different chemical identity.'}

+import hashlib, json, pathlib, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
+
+ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT=ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m34'
+BASE='http://127.0.0.1:8934'
+old=json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m33/root_http.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+jobs=[(x['route'],x['text']) for x in old]
+extra=['Myocet™','My/o/cet','My ocet','Inf/lectra','Equi/dacent','Fy/arro','Megace/ES','Oraqix','Actikerall','Betnesol-n','Maxitrol','Cipro HC','Tobradex','PRED-G','NEO-DELTA-CORTEF','LEP-ETU','Darzalex','Tepezza','Tevimbra','Winrevair','Privigen','Lmx 4','Kadcyla','Tagrisso','Keytruda','Remicade','Tarceva©','Tarceva℠','(+/-)NICOTINE','NICOTINE','ABT-510','ABT 510','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18','MAGE-A3','MAGEA3','Roche','MET','chembl3353410','nct00200395','ensg00000146648','ORG:roche','CHEMBL440464','CHEMBL3']
+jobs += [('/api/explore',t) for t in extra]
+for t in ['What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?','What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?','What trials evaluate NICOTINE?','Which trials evaluate Tobradex?','Which trials evaluate PRED-G?']:
+    jobs += [(route,t) for route in ['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check']]
+rows=[]
+for route,t in dict.fromkeys(jobs):
+    body={'text':t}
+    if route=='/api/structured': body['answer']='{"claims":[]}'
+    if route=='/api/check': body['answer']='NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    started=time.time()
+    req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+route,data=json.dumps(body).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: status=resp.status; result=json.load(resp)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err: status=err.code; result=json.load(err)
+    rows.append({'route':route,'text':t,'request':body,'status':status,'seconds':round(time.time()-started,5),'body':result})
+    if result.get('choose'):
+        for choice in result['choose']:
+            req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+'/api/explore',data=json.dumps({'text':choice['id']}).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: clicked=json.load(resp)
+            assert clicked.get('center',{}).get('id')==choice['id'],(t,choice,clicked)
+            rows.append({'route':'/api/explore','text':choice['id'],'from_choice':t,'status':200,'body':clicked})
+    (OUT/'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+page=urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=30).read()
+(OUT/'root_served_try.html').write_bytes(page)
+summary={'requests':len(rows),'non_200':[{'text':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'status':r['status']} for r in rows if r['status']!=200], 'served_page_matches_file':page==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes(),'page_sha256':hashlib.sha256(page).hexdigest(), 'cases':[{'input':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'result': next((k for k in ['center','refused','alias_only','choose','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in r['body']), 'question'),'center':r['body'].get('center',{}).get('id'),'choices':[c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose',[])]} for r in rows]}
+(OUT/'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in summary.items() if k!='cases'}))

+# Independent relation-map review — round 34
+
+Reviewed **HEAD `1f6017e8ae7923d21cfafca64b1285331e0d2c18`**, parent `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`, on 2026-11-04. **CHANGES REQUIRED: one code blocker.** The original round-33 and round-32 blocker reproductions are closed, but the new refusal check omits stored aliases removed from the lookup index. `Cabozantinib (s)-malate` is refused, while `cabozantinibsmalate` and even `Cabozantinib (s)-malate?` draw its node under the same `_sig`. This violates the explicit requirement that every matching refused stored name remains refused. Separate nonblocking findings concern question recall, chemical punctuation equivalence and source metadata.
+
+The scope is section 0, `POST /api/explore`, and `Engine.explore`, using the round-34 preferred-name policy and [round-32 approval bar](../m32/brief.md). The prior report and repair notes were reviewed. A FIXED disposition closes the exact reproduction under this policy; it does not mean that upstream records were curated or excluded aliases regained recall. No tracked file was edited.
+
+## Verification and evidence
+
+- Ran **`python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`**: **362 passed in 94.65 seconds**, exit 0. Bytecode was disabled; pytest/process temporary paths were under m34. [Output](pytest.txt), [command and timing](pytest_meta.json).

[Raw baseline](root_http.json), [summary](root_confirmation.json), [supplement](root_supplement_http.json). Other lanes have additional HTTP captures.
+- Replayed **4,660 distinct historical/current inputs**: 295 centers, 1,425 refusals, 2,556 `alias_only`, 321 `not_one_name`, 59 `not_found`, four choices. Independent read-name/id membership, ambiguity and choice-set checks found **zero violations and zero exceptions**. [Summary](replay/corpus_summary.json), [all results](replay/corpus_results.json).

All 1,066 drug node labels and indexed preferred names agree with the dictionary's preferred names. This checks recorded membership, not the scientific identity of every possible spelling. [DB oracle](contract/db_validate.json), [DB assertions](contract/db_assertions.json), [UI assertions](contract/ui_assertions.json).
+- Executed the actual served JavaScript in a Node VM DOM/fetch harness, including real HTTP choice clicks, demos, `alias_only`, escaping and history. **No browser rendering result is claimed:** Opera reported no connected browser; two CUA inventory attempts timed out. [Availability record](browser_availability.json), [UI execution](contract/ui_harness_results.json).
+
+The first source oracle intentionally covered `dictionary.index` plus node labels/keys. Its zero failures are valid for that universe but do not cover every spelling in `dictionary.drugs[...].aliases`. An independent final review challenged that assumption. The expanded omitted-alias scan found the blocker below; nine additional root HTTP requests corroborated it. Structural membership passes must not be read as proof of full stored-refusal consistency.
+
+## Code blocker
+
+### M34-B1 — a refused stored alias omitted from the index escapes the refusal closure
+
+The exact stored alias **`Cabozantinib (s)-malate`** is present in `data/processed/resolved.json:1` at **`dictionary.drugs.CHEMBL2103868.aliases[2]`**, with `type: "synonym"`, `source: "ChEMBL"`. Its raw provenance is `data/raw/drugs.json:1`, **`data[563].drug.synonyms[1].label`**. The canonical index keeps only the preferred spelling **`CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE`** for this drug/key. `src/normalize.py:165-171` retains the synonym in the drug's alias list but admits only the first same-drug entry into the index.
+
+`Engine.__init__` at **`src/app.py:61-65`** builds both name tables only from that index. Consequently the stored-spelling check at **`src/app.py:149-154`** never asks the linker about the omitted alias. The ordinary input check still refuses its exact spelling, but other spellings reach the preferred-name admission path.
+
+| Exact HTTP input | Current result |
+|---|---|
+| `Cabozantinib (s)-malate` | `refused`, label `Cabozantinib -malate`, reason `names a distinct product or tracer, not the originator molecule` |
+| `cabozantinibsmalate` | **center CHEMBL2103868 — CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE** |
+| `CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE` | **same center** |
+| `Cabozantinib (s)-malate?` | **same center** |
+| `C a b o z a n t i n i b   ( s ) - m a l a t e` | **same center** |
+| `Cabozantinib/(s)-malate` | **same center** |
+| `Cabozantinib\u200b(s)-malate` (actual U+200B, not literal backslash text) | **same center** |
+
+Every name in the table has `_sig == "cabozantinibsmalate"`. The correct literal trademark control `Cabozantinib (s)-malate™` remains refused, and direct ID `CHEMBL2103868` draws the requested ID as allowed. [Independent full HTTP/source confirmation, exact code points](root_blocker_confirmation.json), [initial independent discovery](contract/omitted_raw_refusals.json), [full omitted-alias scan](attacks/omitted_alias_refusal_summary.json), [all follow-up probes](attacks/omitted_alias_refusal_probes.json).
+
+The full supplement examines **1,652 omitted alias occurrences / 1,376 unique spellings**. Eighteen omitted spellings are linker-refused; six have an indexed equivalent. Of 48 targeted variants, five draw, all in this one Cabozantinib failure family; the root's additional question-mark variant also draws. These counts are not separate blockers. The initial discovery's question-mark input is recorded literally; no converted trademark is misreported as a trademark bypass.
+
+This is a **code/spec consistency failure**, not a claim that the salt name is a different chemical product or that this linker refusal is scientifically correct. The brief's rule is unconditional for stored refused names, and no Cabozantinib exception appears in the recorded exclusions. Repair the refusal-equivalence source so it includes all stored alias spellings, including canonical duplicates omitted from the lookup index, while preserving preferred-name-only admission. No production repair was made.
+

+## New code/design should-fix findings
+
+### M34-S1 — ordinary parentheses or brackets suppress a valid question name
+
+**New, nonblocking question recall regression.** `src/query.py:896-898` excludes parentheses and brackets on either side of a shared-key preferred name. Parent/current comparison and real HTTP show:
+
+| Exact input | Parent | HEAD |
+|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What does (NICOTINE) target?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+| `What trials evaluate [NICOTINE]?` | CHEMBL3, two evidence rows | No entity, zero evidence |
+
+All three question APIs show the loss. Unwrapped `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` still returns two rows; `What trials evaluate (osimertinib)?` still returns 12. A stereochemical prefix attached to a name and punctuation enclosing a name need different treatment. The map correctly leaves these full questions undrawn, so this does not fail the map approval bar. [Parent/current evidence and 45 HTTP calls](contract/boundary_question_comparison.json), [root HTTP corroboration](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+### M34-S2 — the agreed punctuation equivalence can erase stereochemistry
+
+**Nonblocking limitation of the specified `_sig` design, not a newly misfiled alias.** Exact `(+)-NICOTINE` and `(+)NICOTINE` draw **CHEMBL440464, `(+/-)NICOTINE`**. The stored preferred-name record and node label are `(+/-)NICOTINE`; the input is admitted because `_sig` at `src/app.py:261-272` drops the slash and hyphen while retaining `+`, giving `+nicotine` for all three strings. The linker itself returns no entity for the positive-enantiomer forms. [Source record, signatures and HTTP](attacks/stereochemical_punctuation_http.json), [independent root HTTP](root_supplement_http.json).
+
+This is a real chemical distinction: ChEBI identifies `(+)-nicotine` as the R enantiomer, whereas its racemate record contains equal R and S amounts. [ChEBI R-nicotine](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:39162), [ChEBI racemate](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:18723). The review does **not** interpret signature equality as chemical equivalence. This review classifies it as nonblocking under the literal `_sig` admission criterion: these inputs satisfy the stated punctuation equivalence and are not linker-refused names. That is a scope judgment, **not an explicitly recorded stereochemistry exception**. A stronger medicinal-identity guarantee would require changing that comparison policy, preserving stereochemical notation or refusing such normalized matches.
+
+## Data findings, separate from code
+
+### M34-D1 — THEOPHYLLINE is incorrectly typed as Protein upstream
+
+**Nonblocking source metadata error.** Exact input `THEOPHYLLINE` / `Theophylline` draws CHEMBL1355736 with `props.drug_type: "Protein"`. Its raw source is **`data/raw/drugs.json:1`, `data[191].drug`**, containing `id: "CHEMBL1355736"`, `name: "THEOPHYLLINE"`, `drugType: "Protein"`. `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, `dictionary.drugs["CHEMBL1355736"].drug_type`, and the node properties preserve that value. ChEBI independently identifies theophylline as dimethylxanthine, formula C7H8N4O2. [ChEBI identity](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chebi/CHEBI:28177).
+
+The map reproduces the stored value faithfully, and no contrary molecular identity of the named node was established. Therefore this is a curation issue, not a stored-value transcription failure or a wrong-product identity blocker. [Complete source record and response](attacks/theophylline_metadata.json), [HTTP confirmation](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json).
+

+## Integrity and limits
+
+All **7,835 tracked files plus the processed SQLite database and resolved dictionary** match their pre-test SHA-256 baseline: **7,837 files checked, zero changed or added**, tracked git status clean, HEAD unchanged. Both root-owned CLI runs on port 8934 were stopped after their requests completed; all **31 checked owned ports** are closed. [Final integrity](integrity_final.json), [initial hashes](integrity_before.json), [server lifecycles](server_lifecycle.json). Scratch artifacts and this report are confined to m34. No production repair was made, so the user's code-change requirement concerning KOA Studio/KhOpenApi references does not arise.
+
+Changes are required to close M34-B1's omitted-alias refusal gap. Passing tests, the original repaired examples and the indexed-name oracle do not discharge the broader stored-name guarantee. This review also does not certify question free-text refusal completeness, graph-construction brand assignments, upstream source correctness, or chemical equivalence of all punctuation-normalized names.

+import hashlib, json, os, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims' / 'm34'
+
+def hashes():
+    paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf-8').split('\0')
+    extra = ['data/processed/oncokg.sqlite','data/processed/resolved.json']
+    return {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths + extra if p and (ROOT/p).is_file()}
+
+if sys.argv[1] == 'before':
+    (OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'hashes':hashes()},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+elif sys.argv[1] == 'tests':
+    temp = OUT/'tmp'; temp.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    env=os.environ.copy(); env.update(PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',TEMP=str(temp),TMP=str(temp),PYTEST_ADDOPTS='--basetemp="'+str(temp/'pytest')+'"')
+    start=time.time()
+    with (OUT/'pytest.txt').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
+        r=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pytest','tests','-q','-p','no:cacheprovider'],cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+    meta={'command':'python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider','returncode':r.returncode,'elapsed_seconds':round(time.time()-start,3)}

+import hashlib, json, pathlib, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
+
+ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT=ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m34'
+BASE='http://127.0.0.1:8934'
+old=json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m33/root_http.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+jobs=[(x['route'],x['text']) for x in old]
+extra=['Myocet™','My/o/cet','My ocet','Inf/lectra','Equi/dacent','Fy/arro','Megace/ES','Oraqix','Actikerall','Betnesol-n','Maxitrol','Cipro HC','Tobradex','PRED-G','NEO-DELTA-CORTEF','LEP-ETU','Darzalex','Tepezza','Tevimbra','Winrevair','Privigen','Lmx 4','Kadcyla','Tagrisso','Keytruda','Remicade','Tarceva©','Tarceva℠','(+/-)NICOTINE','NICOTINE','ABT-510','ABT 510','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18','FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18','MAGE-A3','MAGEA3','Roche','MET','chembl3353410','nct00200395','ensg00000146648','ORG:roche','CHEMBL440464','CHEMBL3']
+jobs += [('/api/explore',t) for t in extra]
+for t in ['What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?','What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?','What trials evaluate NICOTINE?','Which trials evaluate Tobradex?','Which trials evaluate PRED-G?']:
+    jobs += [(route,t) for route in ['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check']]
+rows=[]
+for route,t in dict.fromkeys(jobs):
+    body={'text':t}
+    if route=='/api/structured': body['answer']='{"claims":[]}'
+    if route=='/api/check': body['answer']='NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    started=time.time()
+    req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+route,data=json.dumps(body).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: status=resp.status; result=json.load(resp)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err: status=err.code; result=json.load(err)
+    rows.append({'route':route,'text':t,'request':body,'status':status,'seconds':round(time.time()-started,5),'body':result})
+    if result.get('choose'):
+        for choice in result['choose']:
+            req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+'/api/explore',data=json.dumps({'text':choice['id']}).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=180) as resp: clicked=json.load(resp)
+            assert clicked.get('center',{}).get('id')==choice['id'],(t,choice,clicked)
+            rows.append({'route':'/api/explore','text':choice['id'],'from_choice':t,'status':200,'body':clicked})
+    (OUT/'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+page=urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=30).read()
+(OUT/'root_served_try.html').write_bytes(page)
+summary={'requests':len(rows),'non_200':[{'text':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'status':r['status']} for r in rows if r['status']!=200], 'served_page_matches_file':page==(ROOT/'web/try.html').read_bytes(),'page_sha256':hashlib.sha256(page).hexdigest(), 'cases':[{'input':r['text'],'route':r['route'],'result': next((k for k in ['center','refused','alias_only','choose','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in r['body']), 'question'),'center':r['body'].get('center',{}).get('id'),'choices':[c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose',[])]} for r in rows]}
+(OUT/'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in summary.items() if k!='cases'}))
